config.\n   472→func cniNamespaceOpts(id string, config *runtime.PodSandboxConfig) ([]cni.NamespaceOpts, error) {\n   473→\topts := []cni.NamespaceOpts{\n   474→\t\tcni.WithLabels(toCNILabels(id, 418→\t\tcontainerd.WithContainerLabels(containerLabels),\n   419→\t\tcontainerd.WithContainerExtension(crilabels.ContainerMetadataExtension, r.meta),\n   420→\t)\n   421→\n   422→\topts = append(opts, containerd.WithSandbox(r.sandboxID))\n   423→\n   424→\topts = append(opts, c.nri.WithContainerAdjustment())\n   425→\tdefer func() {\n   426→\t\tif retErr != nil {\n   427→\t\t\tdeferCtx, deferCancel := util.DeferContext()\n   428→\t\t\tdefer deferCancel()\n   429→\t\t\tc.nri.UndoCreateContainer(deferCtx, r.sandbox, r.containerID, spec)\n   430→\t\t}\n   431→\t}()\n   432→\n   433→\tdefer c.nri.BlockPluginSync().Unblock()\n   434→\n   435→\tvar cntr containerd.Container\n   436→\tif cntr, err = c.client.NewContainer(r.ctx, r.containerID, opts...); err != nil {\n   437→\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create containerd container: %w\", err)\n   438→\t}\n   439→\tdefer func() {\n   440→\t\tif retErr != nil {\n   441→\t\t\tdeferCtx, deferCancel := util.DeferContext()\n   442→\t\t\tdefer deferCancel()\n   443→\t\t\tif err := cntr.Delete(deferCtx, containerd.WithSnapshotCleanup); err != nil {\n   444→\t\t\t\tlog.G(r.ctx).WithError(err).Errorf(\"Failed to delete containerd container %q\", r.containerID)\n   445→\t\t\t}\n   446→\t\t}\n   447→\t}()\n   448→\n   449→\tstatus := containerstore.Status{CreatedAt: time.Now().UnixNano(), Restore: r.restore}\n   450→\tstatus = copyResourcesToStatus(spec, status)\n   451→\tcontainer, err := containerstore.NewContainer(*r.meta,\n   452→\t\tcontainerstore.WithStatus(status, containerRootDir),\n   453→\t\tcontainerstore.WithContainer(cntr),\n   454→\t\tcontainerstore.WithContainerIO(containerIO),\n   455→\t)\n   456→\tif err != nil {\n   457→\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create internal container object for %q: %w\", r.containerID, err)\n   458→\t}\n   459→\tdefer func() {\n   460→\t\tif retErr != nil {\n   461→\t\t\t// Cleanup container checkpoint on error.\n   462→\t\t\tif 466→\t}()\n   467→\n   468→\t// Add container into container store.\n   469→\tif err := c.containerStore.Add(container); err != nil {\n   470→\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to add container %q into store: %w\", r.containerID, err)\n   471→\t}\n   472→\n   473→\tc.generateAndSendContainerEvent(r.ctx, r.containerID, r.sandboxID, runtime.ContainerEventType_CONTAINER_CREATED_EVENT)\n   474→\n   475→\terr = c.nri.PostCreateContainer(r.ctx, r.sandbox, &container)\n   476→\tif err != nil {\n   477→\t\tlog.G(r.ctx).WithError(err).Errorf(\"NRI post-create notification failed\")\n   478→\t}\n   479→\n   480→\tcontainerCreateTimer.WithValues(ociRuntime.Type).UpdateSince(r.start)\n   481→\n   482→\tspan.AddEvent(\"container created\",\n   483→\t\ttracing.Attribute(\"container.create.duration\", time.Since(r.start).String()),\n   484→\t)\n   485→\n   486→\treturn containerRootDir, nil\n   487→}\n   488→\n   489→// volumeMounts sets up image volumes for container. Rely on the removal of container\n   490→// root directory to do cleanup. Note that image volume will be skipped, if there is criMounts\n   491→// specified with the same destination.\n   492→func (c *criService) volumeMounts(platform imagespec.Platform, containerRootDir string, containerConfig *runtime.ContainerConfig, config *imagespec.ImageConfig) []*runtime.Mount {\n   493→\tvar uidMappings, gidMappings []*runtime.IDMapping\n   494→\tif platform.OS == \"linux\" {\n   495→\t\tif usernsOpts := containerConfig.GetLinux().GetSecurityContext().GetNamespaceOptions().GetUsernsOptions(); usernsOpts != nil {\n   496→\t\t\tuidMappings = usernsOpts.GetUids()\n   497→\t\t\tgidMappings = usernsOpts.GetGids()\n   498→\t\t}\n   499→\t}\n   500→\n   501→\tcriMounts := containerConfig.GetMounts()\n   502→\n   503→\tif len(config.Volumes) == 0 {\n   504→\t\treturn nil\n   505→\t}\n   506→\tvar mounts []*runtime.Mount\n   507→\tfor dst := range config.Volumes {\n   508→\t\tif isInCRIMounts(dst, criMounts) {\n   509→\t\t\t// Skip the image volume, if there is CRI defined volume mapping.\n   510→\t\t\t// TODO(random-liu): This should be handled by Kubelet in the future.\n   511→\t\t\t// Kubelet should decide what to use for image volume, and also de-duplicate\n   512→\t\t\t// the image volume and user mounts.\n   513→\t\t\tcontinue\n   514→\t\t}\n   515→\t\tvolumeID := util.GenerateID()\n   516→\t\tsrc := filepath.Join(containerRootDir, \"volumes\", volumeID)\n   517→\t\t// When the platform OS is Linux, ensure dst is a _Linux_ abs path.\n   518→\t\t// We can't use filepath.IsAbs() because, when executing on Windows, it checks for\n   519→\t\t// Windows abs paths.\n   520→\t\tif platform.OS == \"linux\" && !strings.HasPrefix(dst, \"/\") {\n   521→\t\t\t// On Windows, ToSlash() is needed to ensure the path is a valid Linux path.\n   522→\t\t\t// On Linux, ToSlash() is a no-op.\n   523→\t\t\toldDst := dst\n   524→\t\t\tdst = filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Join(\"/\", dst))\n   525→\t\t\tlog.L.Debugf(\"Volume destination %q is not absolute, converted to %q\", oldDst, dst)\n   526→\t\t}\n   527→\t\t// addOCIBindMounts will create these volumes.\n   528→\t\tmounts = append(mounts, &runtime.Mount{\n   529→\t\t\tContainerPath:  dst,\n   530→\t\t\tHostPath:       src,\n   531→\t\t\tSelinuxRelabel: true,\n   532→\t\t\tUidMappings:    uidMappings,\n   533→\t\t\tGidMappings:    gidMappings,\n   534→\t\t})\n   535→\t}\n   536→\treturn mounts\n   537→}\n   538→\n   539→// runtimeSpec returns a default runtime spec used in cri-containerd.\n   540→func (c *criService) runtimeSpec(id string, platform imagespec.Platform, baseSpecFile string, opts ...oci.SpecOpts) (*runtimespec.Spec, error) {\n   541→\t// GenerateSpec needs namespace.\n   542→\tctx := util.NamespacedContext()\n   543→\tcontainer := &containers.Container{ID: id}\n   544→\n   545→\tif baseSpecFile != \"\" {\n   546→\t\tbaseSpec, err := c.LoadOCISpec(baseSpecFile)\n   547→\t\tif err != nil {\n   548→\t\t\treturn nil, 553→\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to clone OCI spec: %w\", err)\n   554→\t\t}\n   555→\n   556→\t\t// Fix up cgroups path\n   557→\t\tapplyOpts := 572→}\n   573→\n   574→const (\n   575→\t// relativeRootfsPath is the rootfs path relative to bundle path.\n   576→\trelativeRootfsPath = \"rootfs\"\n   577→\t// hostnameEnv is the key for HOSTNAME env.\n   578→\thostnameEnv = \"HOSTNAME\"\n   579→)\n   580→\n   581→// platformSpecOpts adds additional runtime spec options that may rely on\n   582→// runtime information (rootfs mounted), or platform specific checks with\n   583→// no defined workaround (yet) to specify for other platforms.\n   584→func (c *criService) platformSpecOpts(\n   585→\tplatform imagespec.Platform,\n   586→\tconfig *runtime.ContainerConfig,\n   587→\timageConfig *imagespec.ImageConfig,\n   588→) ([]oci.SpecOpts, error) {\n   589→\tvar specOpts []oci.SpecOpts\n   590→\n   591→\t// First deal with the set of options we can use across platforms currently.\n   592→\t// Linux user strings have workarounds on other platforms to avoid needing to\n   593→\t// mount the rootfs, but on Linux hosts it must be mounted\n   594→\t//\n   595→\t// TODO(dcantah): I think the seccomp package can be made to compile on\n   596→\t// !linux and used here as well.\n   597→\tif platform.OS == \"linux\" {\n   598→\t\t// Set container username. This could only be done by containerd, because it needs\n   599→\t\t// access to the container rootfs. Pass user name to containerd, and let it overwrite\n   600→\t\t// the spec for us.\n   601→\t\tsecurityContext := config.GetLinux().GetSecurityContext()\n   602→\t\tuserstr, err := util.GenerateUserString(\n   603→\t\t\tsecurityContext.GetRunAsUsername(),\n   604→\t\t\tsecurityContext.GetRunAsUser(),\n   605→\t\t\tsecurityContext.GetRunAsGroup())\n   606→\t\tif err != nil {\n   607→\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate user string: %w\", err)\n   608→\t\t}\n   609→\t\tif userstr == \"\" {\n   610→\t\t\t// Lastly, since no user override was passed via CRI try to set via OCI\n   611→\t\t\t// Image\n   612→\t\t\tuserstr = imageConfig.User\n   613→\t\t}\n   614→\t\tif userstr != \"\" {\n   615→\t\t\tspecOpts = append(specOpts, oci.WithUser(userstr))\n   616→\t\t}\n   617→\t}\n   618→\n   619→\t// Now grab the truly platform specific options (seccomp, apparmor etc. for linux\n   620→\t// for example).\n   621→\tctrSpecOpts, err := c.containerSpecOpts(config, imageConfig)\n   622→\tif err != nil {\n   623→\t\treturn nil, err\n   624→\t}\n   625→\tspecOpts = append(specOpts, ctrSpecOpts...)\n   626→\n   627→\treturn specOpts, nil\n   628→}\n   629→\n   630→// buildContainerSpec build container's OCI spec depending on controller's target platform OS.\n   631→func (c *criService) buildContainerSpec(\n   632→\tplatform imagespec.Platform,\n   633→\tid string,\n   634→\tsandboxID string,\n   635→\tsandboxPid uint32,\n   636→\tnetNSPath string,\n   637→\tcontainerName string,\n   638→\timageName string,\n   639→\tconfig *runtime.ContainerConfig,\n   640→\tsandboxConfig *runtime.PodSandboxConfig,\n   641→\timageConfig *imagespec.ImageConfig,\n   642→\textraMounts []*runtime.Mount,\n   643→\tociRuntime criconfig.Runtime,\n   644→\truntimeHandler *runtime.RuntimeHandler,\n   645→) (_ *runtimespec.Spec, retErr error) {\n   646→\tvar (\n   647→\t\tspecOpts []oci.SpecOpts\n   648→\t\terr      error\n   649→\n   650→\t\t// Platform helpers\n   651→\t\tisLinux   = platform.OS == \"linux\"\n   652→\t\tisWindows = platform.OS == \"windows\"\n   653→\t\tisDarwin  = platform.OS == \"darwin\"\n   654→\t)\n   655→\n   656→\tswitch {\n   657→\tcase isLinux:\n   658→\t\t// Generate container mounts.\n   659→\t\t// No mounts are passed for other platforms.\n   660→\t\tlinuxMounts := 688→\t\tspecOpts, err = c.buildDarwinSpec(\n   689→\t\t\tsandboxID,\n   690→\t\t\tcontainerName,\n   691→\t\t\timageName,\n   692→\t\t\tconfig,\n   693→\t\t\tsandboxConfig,\n   694→\t\t\timageConfig,\n   695→\t\t\textraMounts,\n   696→\t\t\tociRuntime,\n   697→\t\t)\n   698→\tdefault:\n   699→\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported spec platform: %s\", platform.OS)\n   700→\t}\n   701→\n   702→\tif err != nil {\n   703→\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate spec opts: %w\", err)\n   704→\t}\n   705→\n   706→\treturn c.runtimeSpec(id, platform, ociRuntime.BaseRuntimeSpec, specOpts...)\n   707→}\n   708→\n   709→func (c *criService) buildLinuxSpec(\n   710→\tid string,\n   711→\tsandboxID string,\n   712→\tsandboxPid uint32,\n   713→\tcontainerName string,\n   714→\timageName string,\n   715→\tconfig *runtime.ContainerConfig,\n   716→\tsandboxConfig *runtime.PodSandboxConfig,\n   717→\timageConfig *imagespec.ImageConfig,\n   718→\textraMounts []*runtime.Mount,\n   719→\tociRuntime criconfig.Runtime,\n   720→\truntimeHandler *runtime.RuntimeHandler,\n   721→) (_ []oci.SpecOpts, retErr error) {\n   722→\tspecOpts := []oci.SpecOpts{\n   723→\t\toci.WithoutRunMount,\n   724→\t}\n   725→\t// only clear the default security settings if the runtime does not have a custom\n   726→\t// base runtime spec spec. The implication of non-root + caps\n   840→\t// is not clearly defined in Kubernetes.\n   841→\t// See https://github.com/kubernetes/kubernetes/issues/56374\n   842→\t// Keep docker's behavior for out whether we should set no new privilege for sandbox container by default\n   849→\tif securityContext.GetNoNewPrivs() {\n   850→\t\tspecOpts = append(specOpts, oci.WithNoNewPrivileges)\n   851→\t}\n   852→\t// TODO(random-liu): [P1] Set selinux options (privileged or not).\n   853→\tif securityContext.GetReadonlyRootfs() {\n   854→\t\tspecOpts = append(specOpts, 872→\t\t\tspecOpts = append(specOpts, oci.WithBlockIO(linuxBlockIO))\n   873→\t\t} else {\n   874→\t\t\treturn nil, err\n   875→\t\t}\n   876→\t}\n   877→\n   878→\t// Get RDT class\n   879→\trdtClass, err := c.rdtClassFromAnnotations(config.GetMetadata().GetName(), config.Annotations, sandboxConfig.Annotations)\n   880→\tif err != nil {\n   881→\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to Contract\n\nThe CRI defines a stable gRPC interface with 29 RPC methods covering:\n- **Pod Sandbox lifecycle:** Create, start, stop, remove sandboxes\n- **Container lifecycle:** Create, start, stop, remove containers\n- **Execution:** Sync/async exec, attach, port forwarding\n- **Monitoring:** Stats, events, metrics\n- **Configuration:** Runtime status and updates\n\ncontainerd implements this interface through its `criService` in the `internal/cri/server/` package, translating CRI calls to containerd's native APIs (Containers Service, Tasks Service) while managing state through internal stores and integrating with CNI for networking and NRI for resource management hooks.\n\nKubernetes vendors containerd's core API types and supporting packages (errdefs, ttrpc, typeurl) to enable Definitions\n\n#### `api/types/sandbox.proto` (54 lines)\nRepresents sandbox metadata:\n- ID, runtime, spec, labels\n- Timestamps, extensions\n\n#### `api/types/mount.proto` (65 lines)\nDescribes filesystem mounts:\n- `Mount`: type, source, target, options\n- `ActiveMount`: mount with timestamp and mount point metadata\n- `ActivationInfo`: activation state with mounts\n\n#### `api/types/task/task.proto`\nProcess and task metadata:\n- `Status` enum: UNKNOWN, CREATED, RUNNING, STOPPED, PAUSED, PAUSING\n- `Process`: container_id, pid, status, stdio, exit_status\n- `ProcessInfo`: process ID and platform-specific info\n\n#### `api/types/descriptor.proto` (33 lines)\nOCI blob descriptors:\n- media_type, digest, size, annotations\n\n#### `api/types/platform.proto` (30 lines)\nOCI platform specification:\n- os, architecture, variant, os_version\n\n### Service Definitions\n\n#### Containers Service (`services/containers/v1/` - 181 lines)\n\n```protobuf\nservice Containers {\n    rpc Get(GetContainerRequest) returns (GetContainerResponse);\n    rpc List(ListContainersRequest) returns (ListContainersResponse);\n    rpc ListStream(ListContainersRequest) returns (stream ListContainerMessage);\n    rpc Create(CreateContainerRequest) returns (CreateContainerResponse);\n    rpc Update(UpdateContainerRequest) returns (UpdateContainerResponse);\n    rpc Delete(DeleteContainerRequest) returns (google.protobuf.Empty);\n}\n```\n\nCore `Container` message:\n- id, labels, image reference\n- Runtime specification\n- Snapshotter and snapshot key for rootfs\n- Timestamps (created, updated)\n- Extensions map for client-specific data\n- Sandbox association\n\n#### Tasks Service (`services/tasks/v1/` - 227 lines)\n\n```protobuf\nservice Tasks {\n    rpc Create(CreateTaskRequest) returns (CreateTaskResponse);\n    rpc Start(StartRequest) returns (StartResponse);\n    rpc Delete(DeleteTaskRequest) returns (DeleteResponse);\n    rpc Version {\n    rpc Version(google.protobuf.Empty) returns (VersionResponse);\n}\n```\n\n### Supporting Packages\n\n#### errdefs/\nStandard error types mapping to gRPC status codes:\n- Unknown, InvalidArgument, NotFound, AlreadyExists\n- PermissionDenied, ResourceExhausted, FailedPrecondition\n- Conflict, NotModified, Aborted, OutOfRange\n- NotImplemented, Internal, Unavailable, DataLoss, Unauthenticated\n\n#### ttrpc/\nAlternative to gRPC for container runtime communication:\n- 48 Go files implementing timeserving RPC protocol\n- Client, server, channel implementations\n- Unix socket support\n- Streaming and unary RPC capabilities\n\n#### typeurl/v2/\nProtocol buffer Any type marshaling/unmarshaling:\n- Type registry for dynamic type resolution\n- Custom handlers for message serialization\n\n---\n\n## Part 4: CRI to containerd API Mapping\n\n### How CRI Operations Map to containerd Services\n\n| CRI Operation | containerd Service | containerd Method |\n|---------------|-------------------|-------------------|\n| `CreateContainer` | Containers + Tasks | `Containers.Create` + `Tasks.Create` |\n| `StartContainer` | Tasks | `Tasks.Start` |\n| `StopContainer` | Tasks | `Tasks.Kill` |\n| `RemoveContainer` | Containers + Tasks | `Tasks.Delete` + `Containers.Delete` |\n| `ListContainers` | Containers + Tasks | `Containers.List` + `Tasks.List` |\n| `ContainerStats` | Tasks | `Tasks.Metrics` |\n| `ExecSync` / `Exec` | Tasks | `Tasks.Exec` + `Tasks.Start` |\n| `Attach` | Tasks | Streaming via `Tasks.Exec` |\n\n### Request Flow Example: CreateContainer\n\n```\nkubelet\n  │\n  ▼ CRI gRPC Call\nCreateContainerRequest {\n    pod_sandbox_id: \"abc123\"\n    config: ContainerConfig {...}\n    sandbox_config: PodSandboxConfig {...}\n}\n \"xyz789\"\n}\n```\n\n### Data Flow: Pod Sandbox Lifecycle\n\n```\n1. RunPodSandbox\n   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   │ kubernetes (kubelet)                                         │\n   │   └─ CRI Client: RunPodSandboxRequest                       │\n   └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            │ gRPC\n                            ▼\n   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   │ containerd                                                   │\n   │   └─ criService.RunPodSandbox()                             │\n   │       ├─ Generate sandbox ID                                 │\n   │       ├─ Create sandbox lease                                │\n   │       ├─ Setup network namespace (CNI)                       │\n   │       ├─ sandboxService.CreateSandbox()                      │\n   │       ├─ sandboxService.StartSandbox()                       │\n   │       ├─ nri.RunPodSandbox() (NRI hooks)                    │\n   │       └─ Store sandbox in sandboxStore                       │\n   └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n2. StopPodSandbox\n   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   │ containerd                                                   │\n   │   └─ criService.StopPodSandbox()                            │\n   │       ├─ Retrieve sandbox from store                         │\n   │       ├─ Stop all containers in sandbox                      │\n   │       ├─ sandboxService.StopSandbox()                        │\n   │       ├─ Tear down network (CNI)                             │\n   │       └─ nri.StopPodSandbox() (NRI hooks)                   │\n   └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n3. RemovePodSandbox\n   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   │ containerd                                                   │\n   │   └─ criService.RemovePodSandbox()                          │\n   │       ├─ Forcibly stop sandbox                               │\n   │       ├─ Delete lease                                        │\n   │       ├─ Remove all containers                               │\n   │       ├─ sandboxService.ShutdownSandbox()                    │\n   │       ├─ nri.RemovePodSandbox() (NRI hooks)                 │\n   │       └─ Delete from sandboxStore                            │\n   └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n### Key Files Reference\n\n| Component | Location |\n|-----------|----------|\n| CRI gRPC Definition | `/ccb_crossrepo/src/kubernetes/staging/src/k8s.io/cri-api/pkg/apis/runtime/v1/api.proto` |\n| containerd CRI Implementation | `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/internal/cri/server/` |\n| Vendored containerd API | `/ccb_crossrepo/src/kubernetes/vendor/github.com/containerd/containerd/api/` |\n| Vendored Error Definitions | `/ccb_crossrepo/src/kubernetes/vendor/github.com/containerd/errdefs/` |\n| Vendored TTRPC Protocol | `/ccb_crossrepo/src/kubernetes/vendor/github.com/containerd/ttrpc/` |\n\n### Interface Contract\n\nThe CRI defines a stable gRPC interface with 29 RPC methods covering:\n- **Pod Sandbox lifecycle:** Create, start, stop, remove sandboxes\n- **Container lifecycle:** Create, start, stop, remove containers\n- **Execution:** Sync/async exec, attach, port forwarding\n- **Monitoring:** Stats, events, metrics\n- **Configuration:** Runtime status and updates\n\ncontainerd implements this interface through its `criService` in the `internal/cri/server/` package, translating CRI calls to containerd's native APIs (Containers Service, Tasks Service) while managing state through internal stores and integrating with CNI for networking and NRI for resource management hooks.\n\nKubernetes vendors containerd's core API types and supporting packages (errdefs, ttrpc, typeurl) to enable direct communication with containerd daemons through well-defined gRPC/TTRPC service boundaries.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
